		CartJS.cart.custom_item_count = function() {
			var c = 0;
			for (var i=0; i<CartJS.cart.items.length; i++) {
				var item = CartJS.cart.items[i];
				if (item.product_type != "Pocket") {
					c += item.quantity;
				}
			}
			return c;
		}

	function checkUpsellItems(cart) {
		$('.upsell-collection [data-upsell-add] i').text('add_shopping_cart');
		$('.upsell-collection [data-upsell-add] span').text('Add');
		$('.upsell-collection [data-upsell-add]').addClass('btn-outline-primary').removeClass('btn-outline-success');
		//$('.upsell-collection [data-upsell-add]').parent('.col-4').removeClass('order-last');
		for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
			var upSellButton = $('.upsell-collection [data-upsell-add='+cart.items[i].variant_id+']');
			if (upSellButton.length > 0) {
				upSellButton.find('i').text('check');
				upSellButton.find('span').text('in cart');
				upSellButton.removeClass('btn-outline-primary').addClass('btn-outline-success');
				//upSellButton.parent('.col-4').addClass('order-last');
			}
		}
	}

	window.pockets = {
		checking: false,
		queue: [],
		run: function() {
			var item = this.queue.shift();
			console.log(typeof item);
			if (typeof item == 'undefined') {
				console.log('exiting now');
				this.checking = false;
				return;
			}
			/*switch (item.action) {
				case 'add': 
					CartJS.addItem(item.pocket_id, item.quantity, {_pocket_for: item.id}, {success: function() { window.pockets.run(); }});
					break;
				case 'update': 
					CartJS.updateItemById(item.pocket_id, item.quantity, {_pocket_for: item.id}, {success: function() { window.pockets.run(); }});
					break;
				case 'delete':
					CartJS.removeItemById(item.id, { success: function() { window.pockets.run(); }}); 
					break;
			}*/
			switch (item.action) {
				case 'add': 
					CartJS.addItem(item.pocket_id, item.quantity, {_pocket_for: item.id});
					break;
				case 'update': 
					CartJS.updateItemById(item.pocket_id, item.quantity, {_pocket_for: item.id});
					break;
				case 'delete':
					CartJS.removeItemById(item.id); 
					break;
			}
			return;
		}
	};

	function rmerge(arr1, arr2) {
		if (arr2.length == 0) return arr1;
		for(var i in arr2) {
			arr1.push(arr2[i]);
		}
		return arr1;
	}

	function checkPockets(cart) {
		if (window.pockets.checking == true) {
			console.log('Already running checkPockets()');
			return;
		} else {
			console.log('start running now');
		}

		window.pockets.checking = true;
		//window.pockets.queue = [];
		window.pockets.queue.length = 0;

		var withPockets = [];
		var addedPockets = [];

		for (var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
			var item = cart.items[i];
			if (item.properties._pocket) {
				withPockets.push({
					id: item.id,
					quantity: item.quantity,
					pocket_id: item.properties._pocket,
				});
			}
			if (item.properties._pocket_for) {
				addedPockets.push({
					id: item.id,
					quantity: item.quantity,
					item_id: item.properties._pocket_for,
				});
			}
		}

		// pockets to add
		window.pockets.queue.concat()
		var toAdd = withPockets.filter(function (v) {
			for(var i in addedPockets) {
				if (addedPockets[i].id == v.pocket_id) {
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}).map(function(v) { 
			v.action = 'add'; 
			return v; 
		});		
		window.pockets.queue = rmerge(window.pockets.queue, toAdd);

		var toUpdate = withPockets.filter(function (v) {
			for(var i in addedPockets) {
				if (addedPockets[i].id == v.pocket_id) {
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}).map(function(v) { 
			v.action = 'update'; 
			return v; 
		});
		window.pockets.queue = rmerge(window.pockets.queue, toUpdate);

		var toDelete = addedPockets.filter(function (v) {
			for(var i in withPockets) {
				if (withPockets[i].pocket_id == v.id) {
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}).map(function(v) { 
			v.action = 'delete'; 
			return v; 
		});
		window.pockets.queue = rmerge(window.pockets.queue, toDelete);

		window.pockets.run();
	}

	function checkShirts(cart) {
		var hasShirt = false;
		var variantToAdd = [12200784134215];
		var variantToAddFromArray = [12200784134215];
		var alreadyAdded = false;

		for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
			if(cart.items[i].product_type == 'Shirt' || cart.items[i].product_type == 'Boys Shirt' ) {
				hasShirt = true;
			}
			//if(cart.items[i].id == variantToAdd && cart.items[i].properties._freeItem == "true") {
			if(variantToAddFromArray.indexOf(cart.items[i].id) != -1 && cart.items[i].properties._freeItem == "true") {
				alreadyAdded = true;
			}
		}

		if (hasShirt) {
			if (!alreadyAdded) {
				//CartJS.addItem(variantToAdd, 1, properties = {"_freeItem": "true"});
				CartJS.addItem(variantToAddFromArray[Math.floor(Math.random()*variantToAddFromArray.length)], 1, properties = {"_freeItem": "true"});
			}
		} else {
			if (alreadyAdded) {
				for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
					//if (cart.items[i].id == variantToAdd && cart.items[i].properties._freeItem == "true") {
					if (variantToAddFromArray.indexOf(cart.items[i].id) != -1 && cart.items[i].properties._freeItem == "true") {
						CartJS.removeItem(i+1);
					}	
				}
				//CartJS.removeItemById(variantToAdd);
			}
		}
	}

	function checkHasDiscount(cart) {
			var hasDiscounted = false;
			var otherItems = false;
			for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
				if (cart.items[i].properties._discounted) {
					hasDiscounted = true;
				} else {
					otherItems = true;
				}
			}
			if (!otherItems) {
				//console.log('looping');
				if (cart.items.length > 0) {
					CartJS.clear();
					return false;
				}
			}
			return hasDiscounted;
		}

		function checkHasLaundry(cart) {
			var hasLaundry = false;
			var otherItems = false;
			for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
				if (cart.items[i].product_type == "Laundry Bag" && cart.items[i].properties._freeItem == "true") {
					hasLaundry = true;
				} else {
					otherItems = true;
				}
			}
			if (!otherItems) {
				//console.log('looping');
				if (cart.items.length > 0) {
					CartJS.clear();
					return false;
				}
			}
			return hasLaundry;
		}

		function checkHasSocks(cart) {
			var hasSocks = false;
			for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
				if (cart.items[i].properties._regularSocks) {
					hasSocks = true;
				}
			}
			return hasSocks;
		}

		function checkHasFreeSunglasses(cart) {
			var hasFreeSunglasses = false;
			for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
				if (cart.items[i].product_type == 'Sunglasses' && cart.items[i].final_price == 0) {
					hasFreeSunglasses = true;
				}
			}
			return hasFreeSunglasses;
		}

		function checkHasOneShirt(cart) {
			var hasOneShirt = false;
			for(var i=0; i<cart.items.length; i++) {
				if (cart.items[i].product_type == 'Shirt') {
					hasOneShirt = true;
				}
			}
			return hasOneShirt;
		}

	  }

	  }
	}

	var getUrlParameter = function getUrlParameter(sParam) {
	    var sPageURL = window.location.search.substring(1),
	        sURLVariables = sPageURL.split('&'),
	        sParameterName,
	        i;

	    for (i = 0; i < sURLVariables.length; i++) {
	        sParameterName = sURLVariables[i].split('=');

	        if (sParameterName[0] === sParam) {
	            return sParameterName[1] === undefined ? true : decodeURIComponent(sParameterName[1]);
	        }
	    }
	};

	function fireClick(url, params) {
        params['async'] = 'json';
        params['__rwid'] = 1;
        var urlParamsString = Object.keys(params)
            .map(function (x) {
                return x + '=' + encodeURIComponent(params[x])
            })
            .join('&');
        $.getJSON(url + '?' + urlParamsString);
    };

	var __x = getUrlParameter('aff');
	var __y = getUrlParameter('o');
	if((__x != null && __y != null)){ 
		fireClick('https://store.twillory.com', {
        __x: __x, //Replace this with affiliate id
        __y: __y, //Replace this with offer id
        //NOTE: you can add sub values here
        //sub1: 'something'
    	});
	};

  function mergeObject(obj1, obj2){
    var obj3 = {};
    for (var attr in obj1) { obj3[attr] = obj1[attr]; }
    for (var attr in obj2) { obj3[attr] = obj2[attr]; }
    return obj3;
  }
